        proc: subprocess.Popen[str], base_url: str, timeout: float = 5.0
    ) -> None:

    def _wait_for_server(
        proc: subprocess.Popen[str], base_url: str, timeout: float = 5.0
    ) -> None:

        last_error: Exception | None = None

    def _wait_for_server(
        proc: subprocess.Popen[str], base_url: str, timeout: float = 5.0
    ) -> None:
